TL;DR: 本文提出DASSP算法，一种针对约束密度泛函理论（CDFT）中离散化优化问题的单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法。通过子空间分裂重构解耦Stiefel流形约束和非凸二次约束，DASSP是首个具有严格收敛保证的CDFT算法，每步迭代包含谱最小化、投影梯度和阻尼对偶上升三个高效可实现的步骤，在合成和实际CDFT问题上均展现出高可行性精度和良好效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CDFT是描述电子激发态和电荷局域态的强大框架，但其离散化优化问题同时包含Stiefel流形约束和额外的非凸二次约束，现有算法要么无法高精度满足二次约束，要么因双循环迭代结构面临收敛困难，亟需一种能同时保证严格收敛性和高可行精度的算法。

Method: 首先利用旋转不变性并引入非线性子空间对齐约束，推导出子空间分裂重构，将两类约束解耦；基于该重构提出单循环阻尼交替方向乘子法（DASSP），每次迭代由谱最小化步、投影梯度步和阻尼对偶上升步组成，各步骤均具有高效实现。

Result: 在合成和实际CDFT问题上的数值结果表明，DASSP能够达到高可行性精度，且在保持鲁棒性的同时展现出良好的计算效率；同时DASSP是首个对CDFT计算具有严格收敛保证的算法。

Conclusion: 该工作为可靠且高效的大规模CDFT应用铺平了道路，解决了CDFT优化中长期存在的收敛性与精度兼顾的难题。

Abstract: Constrained density functional theory (CDFT) provides a powerful framework for describing electronically excited and charge-localized states, which underlie a broad range of physical and chemical phenomena. However, the discretized optimization problems arising from CDFT calculations remain challenging, owing to the presence of both the Stiefel manifold constraint and additional nonconvex quadratic constraints. Existing algorithms either fail to enforce the quadratic constraints with high accuracy or face convergence issues due to double-loop iterative structures. In this paper, we first derive a subspace-splitting reformulation that decouples the two groups of constraints, by exploiting the inherent rotation invariance and introducing a nonlinear subspace alignment constraint. Based on this reformulation, we propose a single-loop damped alternating direction method of multipliers, called DASSP. To the best of our knowledge, DASSP is the first algorithm for CDFT calculations with rigorous convergence guarantees. Each iteration of DASSP comprises a spectral minimization step, a projected gradient step, and a damped dual ascent step, all of which admit efficient implementations. Numerical results on synthetic and realistic CDFT problems demonstrate that DASSP attains high feasibility accuracy and exhibits favorable efficiency without compromising robustness. We expect that this work will pave the way toward reliable and efficient large-scale CDFT applications.

</details>


### [95] [A proximal subgradient method for nonconvex stochastic optimization under the Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz condition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05460)
*Felipe Atenas,Alejandro Jofré,Pedro Pérez-Aros,David Torregrosa-Belén*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种近端随机次梯度方法，用于最小化期望代价（其被积函数可能非光滑且非凸）与下半连续prox-bounded函数之和。该方法基于非光滑局部化下降引理的结构假设，采用逐步精化的样本平均和Armijo型线搜索，在无需正则器（弱）凸性和随机预言方差一致有界的条件下，建立了函数值几乎必然收敛和聚点平稳性的保证，并利用KL性质将子序列收敛升级为整条轨迹收敛，最后对指数型KL解奇异函数和多项式增长样本量推导出显式多项式收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有随机近端方法通常要求正则器具有（弱）凸性，且需要随机预言方差一致有界，同时样本量需要满足特定的增长率条件。这些限制使得许多实际问题（如被积函数为凸函数差形式、正则器仅prox-bounded等）无法被现有框架覆盖，因此需要一个更一般化的方法框架来统一处理这类问题。

Method: 提出了近端随机次梯度方法：1）将期望代价替换为逐步精化的样本平均值；2）近端次梯度步长通过Armijo型线搜索选择，确保在随机采样误差下仍满足充分下降性质；3）对被积函数施加非光滑、局部化的下降引理变体结构假设（同时涵盖光滑Lipschitz梯度损失和此类损失与凸函数的差）；4）利用KL性质将收敛保证从子序列升级为整体轨迹一致收敛；5）对指数型KL函数和多项式增长样本量推导显式收敛速率。

Result: 建立了函数值序列的几乎必然收敛性和轨迹每个聚点的平稳性，且样本量序列仅需非减无界，无需预设增长率。在KL性质下，实现了整条轨迹收敛到单一平稳点。对指数型KL解奇异函数和多项式增长的样本量，推导了函数值和迭代点列的多项式收敛速率（差一个对数因子）。这些保证即使在光滑情形下也是新的。

Conclusion: 该框架比现有方法适应更广泛的问题形式，无需正则器（弱）凸性和随机预言方差一致有界，收敛保证即使在光滑设定下也具有新颖性，为一般非光滑非凸随机优化提供了全面的理论收敛分析。

Abstract: This work introduces a proximal stochastic subgradient method for minimizing the sum of an expected cost, whose integrand is potentially nonsmooth and nonconvex, and a lower semicontinuous, prox-bounded function. We target a broad class of integrands obeying a nonsmooth, localized variant of the descent lemma in the decision variable, a structural assumption that simultaneously covers smooth losses with Lipschitz gradient and differences of such losses with convex functions. At each iteration the expected cost is replaced by a sample average that is progressively refined, and the proximal-subgradient stepsize is selected by an Armijo-type line search enforcing a sufficient-decrease property up to stochastic errors induced by the sample-based approximation. This framework accommodates substantially more general problem formulations than existing methods, in particular, it requires neither (weak) convexity of the regularizer nor a uniform bound on the variance of the stochastic oracle, and our analysis yields convergence guarantees that are new even in the smooth setting. Specifically, we establish almost sure convergence of the sequence of function values and stationarity of every accumulation point of the trajectories under the relaxed requirement that the sample-size sequence be merely nondecreasing and unbounded, with no prescribed growth rate. Leveraging the Kurdyka-Lojasiewicz (KL) property, we further upgrade this subsequential guarantee to convergence of the whole trajectory to a single stationary point. Finally, for exponential-type KL desingularizing functions and polynomially growing sample sizes, we derive explicit polynomial convergence rates, up to a logarithmic factor, for both the function values and the iterates.

</details>


### [96] [An Inertial Block Proximal Linearized Method with Adaptive Momentum for Nonconvex and Nonsmooth Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05502)
*Weifeng Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种带两阶段自适应动量的惯性块近端线性化方法（IBPL$^+$-TP），用于求解多块非凸非光滑优化问题。该方法引入两阶段自适应动量策略和两个独立无约束的外推点，在保证目标函数单调收敛和序列全局收敛到临界点的同时，在稀疏非负矩阵分解和稀疏非负CP分解等机器学习问题上优于多种现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多块非凸非光滑优化问题广泛应用于地震前异常分析和机器学习等领域，但现有方法在加速收敛方面存在局限性，如外推参数的更新策略不够灵活、外推点受限或参数间存在耦合约束，因此需要设计一种更高效且具有理论保证的求解方法。

Method: 提出了惯性块近端线性化方法（IBPL$^+$-TP），核心创新包括：(1) 两阶段自适应动量策略用于有效更新外推参数；(2) 允许使用两个不同的外推点加速收敛；(3) 两个外推点的外推参数相互独立且不受其他参数约束。同时通过理论分析证明了目标函数的单调收敛性、序列全局收敛到临界点以及收敛速率。

Result: 将该方法应用于两个非凸非光滑机器学习问题（稀疏非负矩阵分解和稀疏非负CP分解，均带$\ell_0$约束），数值实验结果表明该方法在性能上优于多种最先进的方法。

Conclusion: 提出的IBPL$^+$-TP方法在多块非凸非光滑优化问题上具有三方面的优势（两阶段自适应动量、双外推点、参数独立无约束），且兼具严格的理论收敛保证和优异的实际数值表现，是一种有竞争力的优化算法。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of multiblock nonconvex nonsmooth optimization problems, which covers many applications such as the analysis of pre-earthquake anomalies and machine learning. To solve this class of problems, we propose the inertial block proximal linearized method with two-phase adaptive momentum (IBPL$^+$-TP). Compared to the current methods, our method possesses three main advantages: (1) it introduces a two-phase adaptive momentum strategy to effectively update the extrapolation parameters, (2) it allows using two different extrapolation points to accelerate the convergence, (3) it allows the extrapolation parameters of these two extrapolation points to be independent of and unconstrained by all other parameters. While maintaining the above advantages, we prove that our method ensures the monotonic convergence of the objective function of this class of problems, and we also prove that the sequence generated by our method globally converges to a critical point, as well as establish the convergence rate of our method. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, we apply it to solve two nonconvex and nonsmooth machine learning problems, namely sparse nonnegative matrix factorization with $\ell_0$-constraints and sparse nonnegative CP decomposition with $\ell_0$-constraints. The numerical experimental results on solving these problems show that our method outperforms several state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [97] [A Unified Framework for Iterate Convergence of Bregman Proximal Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05536)
*He Chen,Jiaming Fan,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为Bregman近端方法（BPM），包括离散镜像流和连续镜像流，建立了统一的轨迹收敛理论框架。通过扩展缩放Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz（SKŁ）性质并引入与核相关的参数化函数，证明了在广泛的核函数和复合目标函数下BPM的迭代收敛性，并首次在不假设目标凸性或平稳点孤立性的条件下证明了连续时间镜像流的轨迹收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bregman近端方法的迭代收敛性长期以来悬而未决，尤其是对非凸目标而言。现有工作仅针对Shannon熵核和线性约束问题建立了收敛性，缺乏一个适用于更广泛核函数与复合目标函数的统一框架，这是本工作的核心动机。

Method: 本文通过扩展chen2026skl中的分析工具（特别是SKŁ性质），引入与核相关的参数化函数，证明扩展后的SKŁ性质对所有连续半代数函数成立（尤其当核具有闭域时）。在此基础上验证标准BPM在温和正则条件下满足框架假设，从而建立迭代收敛性；同时利用参数化函数证明连续时间BPM（镜像流）在o-minimal可定义目标函数下收敛到平稳点。

Result: 建立了适用于广泛核函数和复合目标函数的统一迭代收敛框架；证明了扩展SKŁ性质对连续半代数函数成立；验证了标准BPM在温和正则条件下满足框架假设；首次在无凸性假设和无平稳点孤立性假设下得到镜像流的轨迹收敛结果。

Conclusion: 本文为离散和连续时间的Bregman近端方法提供了统一的轨迹收敛理论，显著推广了现有结果，填补了非凸设置下BPM迭代收敛分析的空白。

Abstract: Iterate convergence of Bregman proximal methods (BPMs) has long remained open, especially for nonconvex objectives. Recently, \citet{chen2026skl} made progress by establishing iterate convergence for a BPM via the so-called scaled Kurdyka-Łojasiewicz (SKŁ) property, but only for the Shannon entropy kernel and linearly constrained problems. In this paper, we develop a unified iterate convergence framework that applies to a broad group of kernels and composite objective functions. Our approach extends the analytical tools in \cite{chen2026skl}, in particular the SKŁ property, which plays a central role in ensuring convergence of the generated sequences. By introducing kernel-dependent parameterization functions, we show that the extended SKŁ property holds for all continuous subanalytic functions, particularly when the kernel has a closed domain. We then verify that the assumptions of the framework are satisfied by standard BPMs under mild regularity conditions, thereby establishing their iterate convergence for a wide range of objective functions. Furthermore, based on the parameterization functions, we show that the continuous-time BPM (mirror flow) converges to a stationary point for o-minimal definable objective functions, yielding the first trajectory convergence result for mirror flow without imposing convexity assumptions on the objective function or isolation assumptions on stationary points. Taken together, these discrete- and continuous-time convergence results provide a unified trajectory convergence theory for BPMs.

</details>


### [98] [A Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter for Target Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05559)
*Lei Ma,Yuzhong Hu,Xiaoming Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于重定心域的Yau-Yau滤波器（RD-YYF），用于高效非线性目标跟踪。该方法将前向Kolmogorov方程（FKE）的求解限制在以最新状态估计为中心的固定大小局部窗口内，离线阶段使用物理信息神经网络（PINN）生成FKE解快照，并通过主成分分析（PCA）构建低维表示，训练轻量级残差代理模型在线预测密度演化。在两组几何约束目标跟踪实验中，RD-YYF的跟踪误差低于扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）和粒子滤波器（PF），同时保持高效的逐时间步推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau-Yau滤波器将非线性状态估计重构为前向Kolmogorov方程（FKE）驱动的概率密度传播，但在目标跟踪应用中，需要在有限计算域上高效近似FKE。传统全域求解计算代价高昂，难以满足实时跟踪需求，因此需要设计更高效且数值精度有保障的FKE求解方案。

Method: RD-YYF的核心在于：(1) 将FKE求解限定在以最新状态估计为中心的固定大小局部窗口内，集中数值分辨率于主导后验密度区域；(2) 离线阶段利用物理信息神经网络（PINN）生成FKE解快照，主成分分析（PCA）构建密度演化的低维表示；(3) 训练轻量级残差代理模型，将初始条件系数与域中心映射到终端解系数；(4) 在线阶段由预训练代理模型在重定心窗口内预测密度演化，随后进行观测更新与状态估计。

Result: 在两个几何约束目标跟踪实例上，RD-YYF的跟踪误差低于EKF和PF，同时保持了高效的逐时间步推理。消融实验表明，域重定心策略改善了高概率区域的密度近似精度，并加速了离线PINN的收敛。

Conclusion: RD-YYF结合了域重定心、PINN离线训练与PCA低维代理建模，在保证跟踪精度的同时实现了高效的在线推理，展现出其在非线性目标跟踪任务中的应用潜力。

Abstract: The Yau-Yau filter reformulates nonlinear state estimation as probability-density propagation governed by the Forward Kolmogorov equation (FKE). Applying it to target tracking, however, requires efficient FKE approximation on a finite computational domain. This paper proposes a Recentered-Domain Yau-Yau Filter (RD-YYF), which solves the FKE within a fixed-size local window centered at the latest state estimate. This design concentrates numerical resolution near the dominant posterior density. Offline, physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) generate FKE solution snapshots, while principal component analysis constructs a low-dimensional representation of density evolution. A lightweight residual surrogate maps the initial-condition coefficients and domain center to the terminal-solution coefficients. Online, the pretrained surrogate predicts density evolution within the recentered window, followed by observation update and state estimation. Experiments on two geometrically constrained target-tracking examples show that RD-YYF achieves lower tracking errors than the extended Kalman filter (EKF) and particle filter (PF), while retaining efficient per-timestep inference. Ablation results indicate that domain recentering improves density approximation in high-probability regions and accelerates offline PINN convergence. These results demonstrate the potential of RD-YYF for efficient nonlinear target tracking

</details>


### [99] [SDDmiP.jl: A Software Package with a Provably Convergent Benders Algorithm for Multi-Stage Stochastic Mixed-Integer Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05567)
*Akul Bansal,Simge Küçükyavuz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个开源的Benders型分解算法软件包，用于求解多阶段随机整数规划问题。该算法集成了ReLU割以保证混合整数状态变量的收敛性，并提出了两种基于归一化和正则化的割选择策略，以及一种交替割准则，通过四个基准问题类验证了其实际性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多阶段随机整数规划问题的求解具有挑战性，现有的Benders型分解算法虽然有效，但当对偶问题存在多重最优解时，生成的割质量参差不齐，影响算法的收敛效率。需要开发同时保证收敛性和割质量的方法，并提供一个可复现的开源实现。

Method: 实现了一个可证明收敛的Benders型分解算法软件包，包含标准Benders割、强化Benders割、Lagrangian割以及ReLU割（用于处理一般混合整数状态变量的收敛保证）。针对对偶问题多重最优解问题，提出了两种割选择策略：(1)基于对偶问题归一化的策略；(2)基于对偶问题正则化的策略。此外，设计了交替割准则，在Benders割有效时使用计算代价低的割，仅在必要时才调用更昂贵的紧割。

Result: 在四类多阶段随机整数规划基准问题上进行了计算实验，评估了所提出的割选择策略和交替割准则的性能，并分析了问题结构对算法实际性能的影响。

Conclusion: 该开源软件包为多阶段随机整数规划提供了一种可证明收敛且具有实用效率的分解求解方案，割选择策略能够提升割的质量，交替割准则在计算代价和割紧度之间取得了良好平衡，问题结构对算法性能有显著影响。

Abstract: We present an open-source software package that implements a provably convergent Benders-type decomposition algorithm for multistage stochastic integer programs. In addition to standard cut families, such as Benders, strengthened Benders, and Lagrangian cuts, the algorithm incorporates rectified linear unit (ReLU) cuts, which provide convergence guarantees for general mixed-integer state variables. However, the dual problems used to generate these cuts often admit multiple optimal solutions. Although each solution yields a valid cut that separates the incumbent, the resulting cuts can differ in how well they approximate the subproblem cost. To strengthen these cuts, our package implements and evaluates two cut-selection strategies based on normalization and regularization of the dual problem. We also incorporate an alternating-cut criterion that uses cheaper Benders cuts when they are effective and invokes more expensive tight cuts only when necessary. Computational experiments on four classes of multistage stochastic integer programs benchmark these methods and provide insights on how problem structure affects their practical performance.

</details>


### [100] [Convergence Rates for Variational Inequality Projection Neural Networks with a State-Dependent Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05574)
*Mohammed Alshahrani*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究闭凸集上变分不等式的连续时间投影神经网络。针对一种既非Hessian生成亦非标量度量的状态相关矩阵度量，证明了投影关于参数和度量的联合正则性，并在常见谱界下将欧几里得Lipschitz估计从平方界改进为界本身。对Lipschitz强单调算子和Lipschitz度量证明了局部指数收敛（含显式速率和半径）；在紧可行集上借助显式度量变异界获得全局指数收敛。对于二次连续可微、一致正定度量及强单调线性算子，系统存在周期轨道环，且逆度量在整个环上违反Hessian可积性。确定性计算验证了解析公式并量化了收敛证书中的余量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有连续时间投影神经网络求解变分不等式的收敛性分析仅覆盖Hessian生成逆度量与状态相关标量度量两类情形；对于更一般的、状态相关的矩阵度量（其逆既非Hessian生成也非标量），缺乏相应的投影正则性与收敛性理论，亟需填补这一空白。

Method: （1）证明投影映射在其自变量和度量两个参数上的联合正则性；（2）利用谱界条件将欧几里得Lipschitz估计从平方界改进为线性界；（3）对Lipschitz强单调算子与Lipschitz度量推导局部指数收敛的显式速率和收敛半径；（4）在紧可行集上建立显式度量变异界，实现全局指数收敛；（5）构造周期轨道环实例，检验逆度量的Hessian可积性，并通过确定性数值计算验证解析结果。

Result: 建立了广泛状态相关矩阵度量下投影神经网络的收敛性理论框架：局部与全局指数收敛均获证明且具显式参数；同时发现了一种新颖的动力学现象——由强单调线性算子与均匀正定度量共同诱导的周期轨道环，且该构造违反Hessian可积性条件，表明现有Bregman距离分析工具在此类度量下失效。数值实验与解析公式吻合，并量化了收敛证书的保守性。

Conclusion: 该工作将投影神经网络的分析推广到更一般的状态相关矩阵度量类别，既获得改进的Lipschitz估计与显式收敛保证，又揭示了Hessian可积性之外的丰富动力学行为（周期轨道环），为变分不等式问题的优化算法设计提供新的理论支撑。

Abstract: We study continuous-time projection neural networks for variational inequalities on closed convex sets. A positive definite matrix that depends on the state preconditions the operator, and its inverse defines the projection metric. Existing convergence analyses of this flow cover Hessian-generated inverse metrics and state-dependent scalar metrics. In the first case, a Bregman distance eliminates metric-derivative terms. We treat a matrix metric whose inverse is of neither kind. We prove joint regularity of the projection in its argument and metric. Under common spectral bounds, the Euclidean Lipschitz estimate improves from the squared bound to the bound itself. For Lipschitz strongly monotone operators and Lipschitz metrics, we prove local exponential convergence with explicit rate and radius. On compact feasible sets, an explicit metric-variation bound yields global exponential convergence. A twice continuously differentiable, uniformly positive definite metric and a strongly monotone linear operator produce an annulus of periodic orbits. The inverse metric violates Hessian integrability throughout the annulus. Deterministic computations confirm the analytic formulas and quantify slack in both convergence certificates.

</details>


### [101] [An inverse mixed-integer optimization framework for learning interpretable models of expert decision making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05585)
*Anurag Holani,Rishabh Gupta,John Wassick,Qi Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种逆优化方法，通过观测决策者的历史决策，同时学习其感知成本（偏好）和决策规则，从而构建可解释的模型来捕捉和复现专家的决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家的决策策略通常难以显式表达，因为其隐含的偏好和决策规则往往无法被直接说明。学习可解释的决策模型对于培训新手、提升人机系统性能以及实现与人类专家水平相当的全自主系统都具有重要价值。

Method: 采用逆优化（inverse optimization）方法，从专家的历史决策中联合学习两类未知参数：决策者的感知成本（即偏好/主观代价）以及控制其选择的决策规则。

Result: 通过三个案例研究（班次分配问题、生产计划问题和真实路径规划问题）验证了所提方法的通用性。在所有案例中，同时建模感知成本和决策规则相比只建模单一因素能获得更好的预测效果。

Conclusion: 同时学习感知成本和决策规则的逆优化框架具有更强的灵活性，能够更有效地捕捉和复现专家决策行为，验证了该框架的实用价值。

Abstract: Understanding how experts make decisions and being able to transfer that knowledge is important, especially in complex engineering applications. It is highly valuable for training novices, improving the performance of human-machine systems, and potentially enabling fully autonomous systems that perform as well as human experts. However, an expert's decision-making strategy, developed through years of experience, is often not directly accessible, since the implicit preferences and decision rules involved can be difficult to specify explicitly. This has motivated the use of observed decisions made by the expert to learn an interpretable model that captures the expert's decision-making process. In this work, we develop an inverse optimization approach to jointly learn the decision-maker's preferences (or perceived costs) and the decision rules governing their choices. We demonstrate the general applicability of our approach using three case studies that consider a shift assignment problem, a production planning problem, and a real-world routing problem, respectively. Across these case studies, modeling both perceived costs and decision rules leads to better predictions, highlighting the value of the proposed framework and its greater flexibility in capturing and replicating expert decision making.

</details>


### [102] [Fast Gradient Algorithm with Dry-like Friction and Nonmonotone Line Search for Nonconvex Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05653)
*Lien T. Nguyen,Andrew Eberhard,Xinghuo Yu,Chaojie Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Hilbert空间中求解可微（可能非凸）函数最小化的快速梯度算法，通过将凸函数的干摩擦性质推广为非凸设定下的"类似干摩擦"性质，并结合线搜索技术自适应更新参数，实现了到临界点的子序列收敛或全序列收敛，并可在KL性质和Moreau包络下处理弱凸非光滑优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的惯性梯度算法（含Hessian阻尼和干摩擦）在非凸优化中存在参数选择困难、收敛性保证受限等问题，需要一种兼具快速收敛和广泛适用性的算法框架。

Method: (1) 将凸函数的干摩擦性质扩展为非凸设定下的干类似摩擦性质；(2) 采用线搜索技术自适应更新每次迭代的参数；(3) 利用Moreau包络的变分性质将算法适配到弱凸非光滑优化问题；(4) 将凸KL函数Moreau包络的KL指数结果推广到更广泛的非凸、非连续的KL函数类。

Result: 根据参数选择，算法可展示到临界点的子序列收敛或到"近似"临界点的全序列收敛；在KL性质下实现到临界点的全序列收敛；算法可还原为多种现有的含Hessian阻尼和干摩擦的惯性梯度算法；数值实验验证了算法的效率和结合干类似摩擦、外推与线搜索的潜在优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的快速梯度算法在理论上具有灵活的参数选择和多种收敛保证，在实践上能有效求解非凸光滑与弱凸非光滑优化问题，同时统一了多个现有惯性梯度算法框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a fast gradient algorithm for the problem of minimizing a differentiable (possibly nonconvex) function in Hilbert spaces. We first extend the dry friction property for convex functions to what we call the dry-like friction property in a nonconvex setting, and then employ a line search technique to adaptively update parameters at each iteration. Depending on the choice of parameters, the proposed algorithm exhibits subsequential convergence to a critical point or full sequential convergence to an ``approximate'' critical point of the objective function. We also establish the full sequential convergence to a critical point under the Kurdyka--Lojasiewicz (KL) property of a merit function. Thanks to the parameters' flexibility, our algorithm can reduce to a number of existing inertial gradient algorithms with Hessian damping and dry friction. By exploiting variational properties of the Moreau envelope, the proposed algorithm is adapted to address weakly convex nonsmooth optimization problems. In particular, we extend the result on KL exponent for the Moreau envelope of a convex KL function to a broad class of KL functions that are not necessarily convex nor continuous. Simulation results illustrate the efficiency of our algorithm and demonstrate the potential advantages of combining dry-like friction with extrapolation and line search techniques.

</details>


### [103] [Curvature Residual Geometry in Bregman Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05680)
*Vu Khac Ky*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了通过最小化Bregman损失来拟合线性回归模型时，即使生成势函数φ是强凸的，回归目标在θ上也可能非凸的问题。作者推导出Hessian可表示为加权Gram矩阵，并基于此给出局部强凸性、光滑性和梯度下降条件线性收敛的充分条件。针对二次-四次势函数，作者推导了精确的标量凸性条件，识别了负曲率区间，并通过数值实验展示了标量条件与完整Hessian正定性之间的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在反向Bregman回归中，生成势函数的凸性与拟合模型的优化几何之间存在鸿沟——即使φ强凸，关于参数θ的回归目标仍然可能非凸。这一差距使得优化分析变得复杂，作者希望刻画残差依赖的曲率如何与生成势函数和设计矩阵相互作用，从而为非线性回归模型的收敛性分析提供理论基础。

Method: 作者将Hessian矩阵表示为加权Gram矩阵，其中权重依赖于势函数的导数以及当前残差。基于这一表示，推导了局部强凸性、光滑性以及梯度下降条件线性收敛的简单条件。对于二次-四次势函数，推导了精确的标量凸性条件，识别负曲率区间，并给出正曲率的局部和全局充分条件。通过数值实验验证这些条件的行为。

Result: 对于二次-四次势函数，作者获得了精确的标量凸性条件并识别出负曲率的区间。数值实验表明，这些标量条件可能在完整Hessian在评估点仍然正定时就失效了；同时，随着四次项参数增大，导致梯度下降收敛的步长范围缩小。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，反向Bregman回归中残差依赖的曲率与生成势函数及设计矩阵之间存在复杂的相互作用。所推导的条件为理解和保证此类非凸优化问题的收敛性提供了工具，但标量条件与完整Hessian正定性之间的不一致表明需要更精细的分析框架。

Abstract: We consider linear regression models fitted by minimizing Bregman losses of the form \[ \frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^n \left[ φ(y_i)-φ(x_i^\topθ) -φ'(x_i^\topθ) \bigl(y_i-x_i^\topθ\bigr) \right], \] where \(y_i\) is the observed response and \(x_i^\topθ\) is the linear prediction. Even when the generating potential \(φ\) is strongly convex, the resulting regression objective may be nonconvex in \(θ\). This creates a gap between the convexity of the generating potential and the optimization geometry of the fitted model.
  The Hessian can be written as a weighted Gram matrix whose weights depend on the derivatives of the potential and the current residuals. This representation gives simple conditions for local strong convexity, smoothness, and conditional linear convergence of gradient descent.
  For the quadratic--quartic potential, we derive an exact scalar convexity condition, identify the interval of negative curvature, and obtain local and global sufficient conditions for positive curvature. Numerical experiments indicate that these scalar conditions may fail while the full Hessian remains positive definite at the evaluated points. They also indicate that the range of tested gradient-descent step sizes leading to convergence decreases as the quartic parameter grows.
  These results characterize how residual-dependent curvature interacts with the generating potential and the design matrix in reverse Bregman regression.

</details>


### [104] [Quadratic Degree Sequence Optimization and the Critical Roots of a Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05827)
*Frédéric Meunier,Shmuel Onn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了度序列优化问题在二次函数情形下的复杂性，引入图的临界根概念，并证明当二次根变化时，问题的最优值是凸分段仿射函数，其区间由临界根划分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 度序列优化问题是一个基础的组合优化问题，其目标是找一个给定图的子图，使得所有顶点上给定函数（以该顶点在子图中的度数为自变量）的求和最大化。理解其在二次函数情形下的复杂性和结构对于理论计算机科学和组合优化领域具有重要意义。

Method: 文章引入图的"临界根"这一新概念，利用这些临界根将参数空间划分成若干区间，从而分析最优值关于二次根的函数性质。

Result: 证明了当二次函数的根（参数）在实数轴上变化时，该问题的最优值函数是凸的且分段仿射的，其分段点正是由图的临界根所定义的区间。

Conclusion: 通过引入临界根，本文揭示了度序列优化问题在二次函数情形下最优值随参数变化的凸分段仿射结构，为该问题的复杂性分析和算法设计提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The degree sequence optimization problem is to find a subgraph of a given graph which maximizes the sum over all vertices of a given function evaluated at the subgraph degree of that vertex. Here we study this problem and its complexity for quadratic functions. In particular, we introduce the critical roots of a graph, and show they define intervals over which the optimal value of the problem, as the quadratic root varies, is convex piecewise affine.

</details>


### [105] [Robust priority-aware coverage optimization for aerial sensor networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05873)
*Vanshika Datta,C. Nahak,J. C. Yao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向空中传感器网络的优先级感知鲁棒覆盖优化框架，在传感器位置不确定性下最大化加权覆盖，并通过仿真实验验证了其在高优先级区域资源分配上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中传感器网络在实际部署中面临传感器位置不确定性，且不同监控区域具有不同的优先级权重，现有覆盖优化方法未同时兼顾优先级感知与鲁棒性需求，因此需要一种能有效分配传感资源至高优先级区域的优化框架。

Method: 构建了包含监视约束和基于RRF（鲁棒松弛公式）的鲁棒性公式的数学优化模型，并提出了优先级感知鲁棒定向优化（PAROO）算法，用于确定使加权覆盖目标最大化的传感器朝向。

Result: 在机场场景仿真中，该框架有效将传感资源导向高优先级区域，且加权覆盖率优于多种代表性基线方法，展示了在安全敏感环境中的实际适用性。

Conclusion: 所提出的优先级感知鲁棒覆盖优化框架能够在位置不确定性条件下实现加权覆盖的最大化，并在安全敏感场景中优于基线方法，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: This article presents a priority-aware robust coverage optimization framework for an aerial sensor network under sensor location uncertainty. Each region is assigned a priority weight, and the objective is to maximize the weighted coverage while maintaining robustness against positional perturbations. A mathematical optimization model is developed by incorporating surveillance constraints and an RRF-based robustness formulation into the proposed framework. An efficient priority-aware robust orientation optimization (PAROO) algorithm is then proposed to determine the sensor orientations that maximize the weighted coverage objective. Experimental results on an airport-inspired surveillance scenario demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively directs sensing resources toward high-priority regions and achieves higher weighted coverage than representative baseline approaches, highlighting its practical applicability in security-sensitive environments.

</details>


### [106] [Exact Anchoring and a Dualization-Based Matheuristic for Bi-Level Dual-Defense Network Interdiction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05922)
*Wei-Chang Yeh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为双层防御-攻击者模型（BDAM）提供了精确的最优解（首次在测试规模上给出最优参照），以此评估先前混合元启发式算法的解质量。通过下层对偶将主导路径项转化为精确的单层MIP，在全部18个小型基准上确证最优性；同时揭示了供应链规则在并列情形下的病态性，并提出平局不变的威胁走廊公式；最终提出的MILP-DA方法在多数实例上优于已发表的元启发式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层对抗性拦截模型通常用元启发式求解，但在测试规模上缺乏精确最优解，导致解质量无法评估。为了严格检验先前提出的混合元启发式算法在BDAM上的表现，需要提供精确最优解作为参照基准。

Method: （1）对BDAM中占主导的“攻击者路径”项，利用弧长是防御方二元决策的线性函数的性质，通过对下层进行对偶，得到精确的单层混合整数规划（MIP）等价重构；（2）提出“威胁走廊”公式以消除供应链规则在最短路径并列时的平局依赖；（3）构建MILP-DA算法，将精确锚定解与走廊解码及可行性修复相结合。

Result: 在所有18个三行和五行基准配置上确证最优性，其中17个在10秒内求解；在最大15×30网格上得到无证明的强可行解。已发表的平均结果比该可行前沿低3.55%，差距随规模扩大而增大。平局处理使目标值变化小于10^(-3)，但使实际供应成本变化高达0.83单位。MILP-DA在18个已确证配置中的13个及全部18个更大规模实例上优于已发表结果，所有失败均为三行网格。

Conclusion: 精确MIP重构使BDAM的小型基准首次获得验证性最优解；原有的元启发式解质量随规模增大而下降；平局问题虽被目标权重抑制但并未真正消除；MILP-DA混合方法在绝大多数实例上优于原方法，说明将精确思想注入启发式框架可显著提升解质量。

Abstract: Bi-level interdiction models are frequently solved by metaheuristics whose solution quality cannot be assessed, because exact optima are unavailable at the scales tested. We supply them for the bi-level dual-defense attacker model (BDAM), which couples node interdiction, edge destruction and capacitated supply support, and which we previously solved by a hybrid metaheuristic. First, BDAM's dominant attacker-path term admits an exact single-level reformulation by lower-level dualization, a reduction available whenever arc lengths are linear in the defender's binary decisions; the resulting mixed-integer program certifies optimality on all eighteen three-row and five-row configurations of our earlier benchmark, seventeen in under ten seconds, with strong uncertified incumbents out to 15*30 grids. Our published averages sit 3.55% below that frontier, and the gap widens with scale. Second, the supply rule, like any rule priced on a single attacker shortest path, is ill-posed under ties, and our threat-corridor formulation is tie-invariant by construction. The tie-break moves the objective by under 10^(-3) but the realized supply cost by up to 0.83 units, so the defect is suppressed by the objective weight rather than absent. Third, MILP-DA pairs the exact anchor with the corridor decode and a feasibility repair; compared against our published figures with no re-implementation on either side, it wins on thirteen of eighteen certified configurations and on all eighteen larger ones, every loss falling on a three-row grid.

</details>


### [107] [Stabilizer Design for Policy Iteration in Stochastic Linear Quadratic Control: A Spectrum-Assignment Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05953)
*Xinyu Cao,Bing-Chang Wang,Ying Cao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的谱配置方法，用于解决连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中无模型策略迭代（PI）的初始化问题。通过利用Lyapunov型算子的谱信息，从稳定辅助系统逐步逼近原始系统，获得初始稳定控制器，并设计了一种不依赖初始稳定策略的无模型算法实现最优控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 策略迭代是求解最优控制问题的重要强化学习工具，但其初始化阶段（寻找初始稳定控制器）通常依赖完整的模型信息，这严重限制了无模型PI的应用。对于状态和控制都依赖乘性噪声的随机系统，稳定性不再由确定性情况下的Hurwitz条件保证，而是需要满足包含漂移项和扩散项的Lyapunov型不等式，这使得对应的无模型PI初始化问题更具挑战性。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的谱配置方法：(1)利用Lyapunov型算子的谱信息，通过调整累积因子，从稳定的辅助系统逐步逼近原始系统，从而获得稳定控制增益；(2)利用系统数据并调整累积因子，设计了一种不依赖初始稳定策略的无模型算法，可直接实现最优控制。

Result: 通过仿真结果验证了所提出的谱配置方法获得初始稳定控制器的有效性，以及无模型算法在不依赖初始稳定策略条件下实现最优控制的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文成功解决了连续时间不定随机线性二次控制中无模型策略迭代的初始化难题，提出的谱配置方法为随机系统提供了不依赖完整模型信息的稳定控制器获取途径，为无模型强化学习在该类系统中的应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Policy iteration (PI) is an important reinforcement learning tool for solving optimal control problems which includes an initialization stage, i.e., the search for an initial stabilizing controller. However, the initialization stage typically relies on complete model information, thereby imposing substantial constraints on the initialization of model-free PI. For stochastic systems with multiplicative noise dependent on state and control, the stability is not ensured by Hurwitz conditions as in the deterministic case, but rather by a Lyapunov-type inequality that incorporates both drift and diffusion terms. Therefore, the corresponding model-free PI initialization problem is more challenging. To this end, a novel spectrum assignment method is proposed to obtain an initial stabilizer for PI in continuous-time indefinite stochastic linear quadratic control. With the help of the Lyapunov-type operator's spectrum, the original system is gradually approximated from the stable auxiliary system by adjusting a cumulative factor, thereby obtaining a stabilizing control gain. Furthermore, by leveraging system data and adjusting the cumulative factor, we design a model-free algorithm that does not rely on an initial stabilizing policy and can achieve optimal control. Finally, simulation results are provided to validate the effectiveness of the proposed methods.

</details>


### [108] [Distributed Fault Diagnosis in Discrete Event Systems with Transmission Delay Impairments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06035)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了部分可观测离散事件系统中的分布式故障诊断问题，提出了一种考虑传输延迟影响的新的分布式可诊断性条件，并通过延迟记录器和新的诊断函数来验证该条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献中的分布式/分散式可诊断性条件未考虑传输延迟对故障诊断的影响，而实际系统中传输延迟是不可避免的，因此需要建立一种能够在存在传输延迟的情况下验证故障是否可诊断的方法论。

Method: 提出一种新的分布式可诊断性条件，该条件扩展了文献中已有的分散式可诊断性条件；并构建了一个名为"延迟记录器"(delay recorder)的新型结构和一种新的诊断函数，用于验证所提出的分布式可诊断性条件。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法能够成功地判定故障是否可以在有限步数内被诊断。

Conclusion: 本文填补了分布式故障诊断中考虑传输延迟的研究空白，提供了在传输延迟存在情况下验证故障可诊断性的有效方法论。

Abstract: This note studies the distributed fault diagnosis problem in partially-observed discrete event systems, where the system is monitored by a group of agents to cooperatively diagnose faults within a finite number of steps. The novelty of this work is the creation of a methodology to verify when the faults can be diagnosed even in the presence of transmission delay impairments. To address this scenario, a new distributed diagnosability condition is proposed, which extends decentralized diagnosability conditions proposed in the literature. Such distributed diagnosability condition is then verified via a novel structure named delay recorder and a new diagnosis function. Theoretical analysis shows that the verification method can successfully determine whether the faults can be diagnosed.

</details>


### [109] [New Lower Bounds for Weak Limited Augmented Zarankiewicz Numbers in the $m\times 3$ Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06050)
*Liqun Qi,Yannan Chen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文确定了弱有限增广Zarankiewicz数z_wL(m,3)在m=5,6,7,8,9时的精确值，全部为2m，并通过显式弱容许构造和精确有限搜索给出了下界与匹配的上界，强力支持z_wL(m,3)=2m对所有m≥5成立的猜想，同时推导出BSR(m,3)≥2m。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定弱有限增广Zarankiewicz数在特定参数下的精确值，验证并支持关于z_wL(m,3)=2m（m≥5）的猜想，并由此获得相关BSR（双向分解）问题的下界。

Method: 显式弱容许构造提供下界；对m=7,8,9采用在规范C_4-free基图上的精确有限搜索获得匹配上界。

Result: z_wL(5,3)=10, z_wL(6,3)=12, z_wL(7,3)=14, z_wL(8,3)=16, z_wL(9,3)=18，即z_wL(m,3)=2m对5≤m≤9成立；同时推出BSR(m,3)≥2m（m=5,6,7,8,9）。

Conclusion: 实验结果强烈支持z_wL(m,3)=2m对所有m≥5成立的一般性猜想，并为BSR(m,3)提供了新的下界。

Abstract: We determine the exact weak limited augmented Zarankiewicz numbers $z_{wL}(m,3)$ for $m=5,6,7,8,9$. We establish \[ z_{wL}(5,3)=10,\quad z_{wL}(6,3)=12,\quad z_{wL}(7,3)=14,\quad z_{wL}(8,3)=16,\quad z_{wL}(9,3)=18. \] Thus $z_{wL}(m,3)=2m$ for all $5\le m\le 9$. The lower bounds are given by explicit weak admissible constructions, while the matching upper bounds for $m=7,8,9$ are obtained by exact finite searches over canonical $C_4$-free base graphs. Together with the known small cases, this strongly supports the conjecture that \[ z_{wL}(m,3)=2m \quad \text{for all } m\ge 5. \] Since BSR$(m,n)\ge z_{wL}(m,n)$, these constructions imply \[ {\rm BSR}(m,3)\ge 2m \quad \text{for } m=5,6,7,8,9. \]

</details>


### [110] [On Same-Sample and Independent-Sample Stochastic Extragradient for Monotone Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06182)
*TaeHo Yoon,Nicolas Loizou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了随机外梯度法（SEG）在单调变分不等式问题中的收敛性，重点比较了同样本SEG（S-SEG）与独立样本SEG（I-SEG）两种变体，首次揭示了S-SEG对逐样本Lipschitz参数的敏感性，并在放宽假设条件下建立了高概率受限间隙收敛性，同时证明了某些基本改进在一般情况下不可能实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外梯度法是求解变分不等式的基础算法，其确定性收敛理论已相当成熟，但随机版本的收敛行为仍未被充分理解。现有研究大多聚焦于独立样本SEG且假设域紧致或随机算子方差一致有界，而同样本SEG这一性质截然不同的自然变体受到的关注远不足。本文旨在弥合这一文献空白。

Method: 本文采用理论分析方法：（1）构造反例证明S-SEG对逐样本Lipschitz参数敏感，仅凭均值Lipschitz性和有界方差不足以确保其在紧致集上收敛；（2）在放宽的假设条件下（允许无界域），为SEG的两种变体建立高概率受限间隙收敛结果，并论证这些结果在一般情况下不可改进；（3）通过构造随机单调VIP反例，证明对I-SEG有效的非对称双重步长选择在S-SEG下会几乎必然发散。

Result: （1）证明S-SEG的收敛性依赖于逐样本Lipschitz条件，这是比I-SEG更严格的要求；（2）在放宽假设下建立了SEG变体的高概率受限间隙收敛界，并证明了其最优性；（3）发现已知的非对称双重步长策略对S-SEG失效，存在S-SEG在该策略下几乎必然发散的随机单调VIP实例。

Conclusion: S-SEG与I-SEG在收敛行为上存在本质差异：S-SEG需要更严格的假设条件（逐样本Lipschitz性），且某些对I-SEG有效的步长策略可能导致S-SEG发散。这为理解和设计适用于不同场景的随机外梯度算法提供了更完整的理论图景。

Abstract: We study stochastic extragradient (SEG) methods for solving monotone variational inequality problems (VIPs) over a feasible set. Although extragradient is a foundational algorithm for VIPs and its deterministic convergence theory is well developed, its stochastic counterpart remains less understood. Most existing analyses focus on independent-sample SEG (I-SEG) and assume either that the domain is compact or that the variance of the stochastic operator is uniformly bounded. The behavior of same-sample SEG (S-SEG), a natural variant with materially different properties, has received far less attention. In this work, we address these gaps in the literature. We first show that S-SEG is sensitive to samplewise Lipschitz parameters: mean Lipschitzness and bounded variance alone do not ensure convergence, even on a compact set. Then, for possibly unbounded domains, we establish a high-probability restricted-gap convergence for each SEG variant under a relaxed set of assumptions, and show that certain fundamental improvements to these results are impossible in general. Finally, we show that a known asymmetric double step-size selection that guarantees almost sure last-iterate convergence for I-SEG can fail for S-SEG: there exists a stochastic monotone VIP for which S-SEG diverges almost surely even under the modified step-sizes.

</details>


### [111] [Characteristic Sensitivity Ensembles for Inference of Hidden Dynamics from Marginal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06190)
*Qi Wang,Gustaaf Jacobs*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种从边缘概率密度函数推断广义常微分方程组动力学参数的框架，通过将扩散等不可逆过程重构为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，并利用特征线表示结合交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计量，实现随机梯度下降的参数推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多实际动力学过程（如扩散和其他不可逆随机过程）在低维观测中难以直接推断，传统方法受限于高维Liouville方程的求解困难。作者希望通过将随机/不可逆动力学重构为增广状态空间中的确定性可逆流，从而将不可逆动力学推断问题转化为可处理的常微分方程参数恢复问题。

Method: 提出一种随机梯度方法：将低维观测的边缘概率密度函数与潜在变量的联合密度联系起来，利用增广状态空间中的双曲Liouville方程描述确定性可逆流。算法不直接求解高维Liouville方程，而是利用其特征表示——从初始分布采样的粒子沿特征线输运，并通过沿特征线的敏感性传播获得欧拉灵敏度。采用交叉U统计量构造无偏梯度估计量，驱动随机梯度下降。

Result: 通过四个实验验证了方法有效性：(1)三模态线性系统从单模态边缘观测中恢复参数；(2)带隐藏模态的非线性Gompertz增长模型；(3)隐藏模态使单峰边缘分布变为双峰的双稳态系统；(4)细胞流中颗粒的Stokes-Oseen阻力定律恢复。各设置下均分析了收敛行为。

Conclusion: 该框架成功地将不可逆随机动力学的推断问题转化为增广空间中确定性ODE的参数恢复问题，通过特征线表示和U统计量梯度估计绕开了高维Liouville方程的数值求解瓶颈，为一般化ODE系统的动力学推断提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: A framework is developed for the inference of dynamics described by a generalized system of ordinary differential equations. A stochastic gradient method is coined that infers dynamics from observed marginal probability density functions using the joint probability density function of the observable and latent variables. Diffusion and other irreversible processes observed in a low-dimensional state can be recast as deterministic, reversible flows in a sufficiently augmented state space, where the joint density satisfies the hyperbolic Liouville equation. The marginal distribution observed is the projection of these hyperbolic dynamics onto the observed coordinates, with the latent components carrying the randomness and memory. This reframing allows inference for irreversible or stochastic dynamics into the recovery of a deterministic Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) from marginal observations. Instead of solving the high-dimensional Liouville equation for the joint density, the algorithm exploits its characteristic representation. Particles sampled from the initial distribution are transported along characteristic lines. The Eulerian sensitivity with respect to parameters is obtained by sensitivity propagation along the characteristic lines, with a crossed U-statistic producing an unbiased gradient estimator, which enables stochastic gradient descent. Four experiments validate the method: recovery of a three-mode linear system observed through the marginal of a single mode; a nonlinear Gompertz growth model with a hidden mode; a bistable system whose hidden mode turns a unimodal marginal bimodal; and Stokes--Oseen drag law recovery for particles in a cellular flow. Convergence behavior is analyzed across these settings.

</details>


### [112] [Muon on the Stiefel Manifold Admits an Exact Closed-Form Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06218)
*Mikhail Solonko,Molozhavenko Alexander,Maxim Rakhuba*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Muon优化方法在Stiefel流形上的扩展，证明了其更新具有精确闭式解，并据此开发了名为Skewon的高效算法，同时建立了其在光滑非凸设定下的一阶收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon是一种矩阵感知的优化方法，但现有的将其扩展到Stiefel流形（由正交列矩阵构成，在机器学习和科学计算中广泛应用）的方案依赖于启发式、近似或迭代更新，计算效率各不相同，缺乏精确且高效的解决方案。

Method: 作者证明了Stiefel Muon更新存在精确的闭式解，基于此开发了Skewon算法，该算法具有高效的实现方式，并针对光滑非凸设定建立了Skewon的一阶收敛性质。

Result: 提出了Skewon，一个面向正交约束优化的实用算法，具有高效实现和精确闭式更新，同时提供了在光滑非凸条件下的首次收敛保证。

Conclusion: Stiefel Muon更新可以精确求解而非近似，由此得到的Skewon算法在效率、精确性和理论保证方面均优于现有启发式扩展方法。

Abstract: We study Muon, a recently proposed matrix-aware optimization method, in the context of the Stiefel manifold. This manifold consists of matrices with orthonormal columns and is ubiquitous in machine learning and scientific computing. Existing extensions of Muon to this manifold rely on heuristic, approximate, or iterative updates with varying computational efficiency. We show that the corresponding Stiefel Muon update admits an exact closed-form solution and use this result to develop Skewon, a practical algorithm for orthogonality-constrained optimization with an efficient implementation. We further establish first-order convergence guarantees for Skewon in the smooth non-convex setting.

</details>


### [113] [Localized Stabilization of Transport PDEs by Interior Flux Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06249)
*Constantinos Kitsos,Ian Manchester*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究多维连续方程在有界域上通过局部内部通量反馈实现稳定化的问题。反馈通过通量的散度施加，使误差相对于参考剖面满足带局部阻尼的输运方程。在有限时间特征阻尼不等式和增益条件下，证明了误差在L2中的指数稳定性，并给出了加权Lyapunov泛函、输入到状态（ISS）估计、椭圆右逆实现以及非线性速度场的推广，最后通过二维例子验证了相关条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对多维连续方程（transport equations）在有界域上的稳定控制问题，现有研究缺乏对局部内部通量反馈的系统性理论框架。本文旨在通过通量散度的局部反馈设计实现误差的指数稳定，并建立相应的几何与增益条件。

Method: (1) 通过通量散度施加局部内部反馈，使误差满足带局部阻尼的输运方程；(2) 引入有限时间特征阻尼不等式作为主要几何条件；(3) 利用Lyapunov型入口条件保证支持受限轨迹族上的特征阻尼；(4) 构造加权Lyapunov泛函，得到微分Lyapunov准则和ISS估计；(5) 讨论反馈通量的椭圆右逆实现；(6) 将特征阻尼论证推广到状态非线性依赖的速度场。

Result: 在增益条件（连接局部阻尼与输运场压缩放大的关系）下，证明了误差在L2空间中具有指数稳定性；建立了带加性扰动的输入到状态（ISS）估计；给出了反馈通量的椭圆右逆实现方案；通过二维例子验证了几何条件、增益条件和实现条件的可行性。

Conclusion: 本文建立了多维连续方程通过局部内部通量反馈实现稳定化的完整理论框架，包括几何条件、Lyapunov分析、ISS估计以及实际实现途径，为更广泛的多维输运方程的反馈镇定提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study stabilization of multidimensional continu- ity equations with source terms on bounded domains by means of localized interior flux feedback. The feedback is prescribed through the divergence of the flux and is chosen so that the error with respect to a reference profile satisfies a transport equation with localized damping. The main geometric condition is a finite-time characteristic damping inequality, requiring relevant characteristics to accumulate a uniform amount of damping over a time horizon. This condition is shown to yield exponential stability in L2 of the error, under a gain condition relating localized damping to compressive amplification of the transport field. Lyapunov-type entrance conditions ensure characteris- tic damping on support-restricted families of trajectories. A weighted Lyapunov functional provides a differential Lyapunov criterion and an input-to-state (ISS) estimate with respect to additive perturbations. We also discuss elliptic right-inverse realizations of the feedback flux and extend the characteristic damping argument to velocity fields depending nonlinearly on the state. A two-dimensional example finally illustrates the geometric, gain, and realization conditions.

</details>


### [114] [Fault Diagnosis and Prognosis in Partially-Observed Discrete Event Systems with Delayed Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06295)
*Jiwei Wang,Simone Baldi,Wenwu Yu,Xiang Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究离散事件系统中观测延迟场景下的故障诊断与预测问题，提出了针对延迟观测的可诊断性和可预测性新条件，并引入了延迟观测器与新的验证函数来检验这些条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散事件系统的故障诊断与预测方法通常假设观测是即时到达的，但在实际系统中观测可能延迟。当存在观测延迟时，现有的可预测性（prognosability）定义过于保守，需要修订，因此需要提出新的诊断和预测条件来应对这一场景。

Method: 首先提出了两个针对延迟观测的诊断与预测条件；然后引入了一种延迟观测器（delay observer）来构建系统状态估计；最后设计了一个新的验证函数来检验所提出的可诊断性和可预测性条件。

Result: 理论分析表明，所提出的验证方法能够有效地用于离散事件系统中的故障诊断与预测，并且修订后的可预测性概念避免了原定义在延迟观测场景下的保守性问题。

Conclusion: 本文为延迟观测下的离散事件系统故障诊断与预测提供了理论框架和验证工具，修订可预测性定义并引入延迟观测器与验证函数，克服了状态最先进方法在此场景下的保守性局限。

Abstract: Fault diagnosis and prognosis in discrete event systems are studied in the scenario where the observations are possibly received with delay. To address this scenario, two conditions for diagnosis and prognosis with delayed observations are proposed, where we show that the state-of-the-art notion of prognosability must be revised to avoid conservativeness. Diagnosability and prognosability conditions are then verified by introducing a delay observer and a new verification function. Theoretical analysis indicates the effectiveness of the verification method for fault diagnosis and prognosis in the system.

</details>


### [115] [The Benefits of an Integrated Approach for Stability-Constrained Power System Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06349)
*Gereon Recht,Benedikt Jahn,Oussama Alaya,Karl-Kiên Cao,Hendrik Lens*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了电力系统规划中集成式方法相较于传统顺序式方法的优势，即将惯性响应和动态电压稳定约束纳入发电、输电和储能系统扩建的统一优化框架中。研究发现集成式方法可降低系统总成本，且网格构成型电池储能系统的双重用途优于静态同步补偿器等其他稳定措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着逆变器型资源（IBR）在电力系统中的渗透率不断提高，需要替代同步发电机对动态电压稳定性和惯性响应的贡献。然而，传统的电力系统规划方法是顺序式的，稳定性措施仅在规划过程的后期阶段才被考虑，这可能导致次优的规划结果和更高的系统成本。

Method: 提出一种集成的电力系统规划方法，在发电、输电和储能系统扩建的优化模型中同时纳入简化的稳定性约束（惯性约束和电压稳定约束），将稳定性措施与其余规划决策在同一框架内联合优化。

Result: 集成式规划方法能够降低系统总成本；网格构成型电池储能系统因其双重用途（既提供稳定服务又参与能量调度）而优于静态同步补偿器等单一用途的稳定措施。

Conclusion: 将稳定性约束纳入电力系统规划的前期阶段（即集成式规划）相比顺序式规划更具经济优势，且网格构成型电池储能系统是成本效益最优的稳定措施选择。

Abstract: Increasing penetration of inverter-based resources in today's power systems requires substitution of the contribution from synchronous generators to dynamic voltage stability and inertial response. However, established approaches for power system planning are sequential, as stabilising measures are only considered at a later stage of the planning process. We investigate the advantages of an integrated approach for power system planning, where stabilising measures are considered simultaneously with the expansion of generation, transmission, and storage systems via simplified stability constraints on inertia and voltage stability. We find that system costs are reduced with the integrated approach and that the dual-use option of grid-forming battery energy storage systems is favoured over other stabilising measures like static synchronous compensators.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [MS-MLB: An Open Machine Learning Benchmark for Blood-Based MS Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05196)
*Adam Simson,Ankush Dutta,Quang Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MS-MLB（多发性硬化机器学习基准），这是一个可复现的开放基准，用于从全血RNA表达数据中对多发性硬化与健康对照进行分类。使用公开的GSE17048队列，在共享的无泄漏流水线下评估多种机器学习算法，包含嵌套交叉验证、独立的留出集、bootstrap置信区间、ROC/PR分析、校准度量及探索性MS研究评分。梯度提升在留出集上排名第一，MS研究评分为93.83，AUC-ROC为0.989。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多发性硬化的诊断依赖临床评估、MRI和实验室检查，血液RNA表达数据可能包含疾病相关的免疫信号，但缺乏一个可复现、无泄漏的开放基准来公平评估机器学习方法在这一分类任务上的表现。

Method: 使用公共GSE17048队列构建MS与健康对照分类任务，在共享且无泄漏控制的流水线下评估多种算法；评估方案包括嵌套交叉验证、独立的留出集、bootstrap置信区间、ROC和PR分析、校准测量以及探索性MS研究评分；并提供文档化的外部模型提交通道。

Result: 梯度提升在留出集上以MS研究评分93.83排名第一，AUC-ROC为0.989，灵敏度0.950，特异度0.778，F1分数0.927，Brier分数0.050。

Conclusion: MS-MLB是首个针对GSE17048全血RNA表达数据、聚焦MS与健康对照分类的开放基准，带有内置的外部模型提交通道。研究评分仅用于研究比较，尚未经过临床验证，不能替代临床诊断。

Abstract: Multiple sclerosis (MS) is diagnosed through clinical assessment, magnetic resonance imaging, laboratory evidence when appropriate, and exclusion of better explanations. Blood RNA expression data may contain disease associated immune signal, but a blood RNA classifier cannot be treated as a replacement for clinical diagnosis. This paper presents MS-MLB (Multiple Sclerosis Machine Learning Benchmark), a reproducible open benchmark for machine learning based MS research classification from whole blood RNA expression data. MS-MLB uses the public GSE17048 cohort, converts it into an MS versus healthy control task, and evaluates multiple algorithms under a shared, leakage controlled pipeline that a researcher can rerun without reconfiguring the evaluation. The evaluation includes nested cross-validation, an untouched stratified holdout set, bootstrap confidence intervals, ROC and precision recall analysis, calibration measurement, and an exploratory MS Research Score. In the final benchmark summary, Gradient Boosting ranked first by MS Research Score on the holdout set, with an MS Research Score of 93.83, AUC-ROC of 0.989, sensitivity of 0.950, specificity of 0.778, $F_{1}$ score of 0.927, and Brier score of 0.050. Prior studies have applied machine learning to MS blood transcriptomic data, including PBMC stage classification and whole blood diagnostic signature modeling. The contribution here is different and narrower. To our knowledge, MS-MLB is the first open benchmark focused on MS versus healthy control classification from GSE17048 whole blood RNA expression data with a documented external model submission pathway built into the framework. The score is intended for research comparison only and has not been clinically validated. The benchmark is accessible here: https://github.com/duckyquang/MS-MLB.

</details>


### [117] [When Do Corrective Features Help? An Agent for Corrective Feature Discovery on Black-Box Forecasters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05207)
*Fangxin Wang,Ziyi Zhang,Diyi Zhuang,Langzhou He,Shiyu Wang,Baichuan Mo,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFTER框架，通过从冻结预训练预报器的残差中挖掘可解释修正特征进行轻量级后校正。它结合组合搜索和LLM两个互补生成器，在六个公开数据集和六个冻结骨干网上超越所有专用特征工程系统，且误差最高降低27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结的预训练预测模型会以结构化、重复的方式失败，而微调修复代价高昂。已有自动特征工程方法建模的是数据生成过程，而非模型失败过程，因此需要一种新的纠正性特征发现方法。

Method: CRAFTER保持骨干网冻结，从残差中挖掘特征，使用两个互补生成器：一是对原始输入通道进行组合搜索，二是利用LLM提出命名特征组合、二值标志和短可执行代码。所有候选特征通过统一的验证集门控机制被接受或拒绝，最后由验证集选择的修正器应用被接受的特征，或保持预测不变。该源无关的流水线允许在相同条件下评估先前的特征工程系统。

Result: 在六个公开数据集和六个冻结骨干网上，CRAFTER在每种特征预算下均超越所有专用特征工程系统，比仅用修正器的方法提升约一倍，使最弱骨干网的误差降低最高达27%。这些增益在不同LLM后端下保持稳健，且在微调骨干网上仍然有效。

Conclusion: CRAFTER证明了纠正式残差特征发现的有效性，其源无关设计使得特征来源成为改进归因的唯一变量，为评估不同特征工程系统提供了统一基准，并展示了LLM与组合搜索互补的潜力。

Abstract: Frozen pretrained forecasters often fail in structured, recurring ways that are costly to repair through fine-tuning. We study corrective feature discovery: mining interpretable features of a frozen forecaster's residual to drive a lightweight post-hoc corrector. Prior automated feature engineering models the data-generating process; corrective features instead model the model-failure process. We present CRAFTER (Corrective Residual Agent with Feature-based Temporal Exploration and Reasoning), which keeps the backbone frozen and mines its residual with two complementary generators: a compositional search over the raw input channels, and a large language model (LLM) that proposes named feature combinations, binary flags, and short executable code. A single validation-grounded gate accepts or rejects every candidate regardless of its origin, and a validation-selected corrector applies the accepted features or leaves the forecast unchanged. This source-agnostic pipeline also allows prior feature-engineering systems to be evaluated under identical conditions, making CRAFTER an instrument for attributing forecast improvements to the feature source alone. Across six public datasets and six frozen backbones, CRAFTER surpasses every dedicated feature-engineering system at every feature budget, roughly doubling the improvement achieved by the corrector alone and reducing the error of the weakest backbones by up to 27%. These gains are robust across different LLM backends and persist even when applied on top of fine-tuned backbones.

</details>


### [118] [PPDL: LLM-Based Flows as Probabilistic Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05234)
*Louis Mandel,Guillaume Baudart,Mandana Vaziri,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于编程基于LLM流程的概率语言，使开发者能够量化并传播不确定性，同时无需修改流程逻辑即可实验不同的推理缩放技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的应用在可靠性方面仍面临重大挑战：LLM输出常缺乏准确性和置信度度量，而在多步LLM调用和工具组合的流程中，这种不确定性会累积放大，使开发者和终端用户难以信任结果。

Method: 提出一种概率语言，用于编程LLM-based flows。该语言允许开发者量化不确定性并沿着应用流程传播，同时支持无需更改流程代码即可实验不同的推理缩放（inference scaling）技术。

Result: 通过实验研究展示了该能力的有效性，并通过一个面向Rocq定理证明器的定理证明智能体案例研究进行了验证。

Conclusion: 概率语言为编程LLM流程提供了一种系统化的不确定性量化与传播方法，有望提升基于LLM应用的可信度和可靠性。

Abstract: Building reliable applications that leverage large language models (LLMs) remains a significant challenge. While LLMs offer impressive capabilities across diverse tasks, their outputs often lack accuracy and provide no clear measure of confidence. This uncertainty compounds in flows of multiple calls to LLMs and other tools, making it difficult for developers and end-users to trust the results. This paper introduces a probabilistic language for programming LLM-based flows. It enables developers to quantify and propagate uncertainty throughout the application's flow, and experiment with different inference scaling techniques without adding a single line of code beyond the flow's logic. We present an experimental study to demonstrate this capability, and a case study building a theorem proving agent for the Rocq theorem prover.

</details>


### [119] [Decoupling Perception from Description: Computation-Grounded Representation Alignment between Multivariate Time Series and Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05238)
*Xinran Feng,Yi Xie,Chao Zhang,Ruikun Li,Wanyun Ling,Ziyue Li,Chenxi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CGTime，一个4B参数的计算接地（computation-grounded）时间序列-语言模型。它通过确定性代码预先计算多变量时间序列的统计信息，再让LLM对这些事实进行语言化描述，从而将感知与描述解耦，突破了自监督陷阱——即用LLM生成标签的范式无法超越标注者自身感知能力的天花板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练多模态模型对齐时间序列与语言面临两个核心问题：(1) 自监督陷阱——用LLM读取序列并生成描述时，标签质量受限于模型自身的感知能力，数据永远无法教会模型超越标注者的知识；(2) 数据缺口——大多数数据集仅包含单变量，但真正重要的模式（跨通道相关、领先-滞后结构、共现异常）只出现在多变量场景中，恰好是LLM感知极限最容易暴露的地方。这两个问题构成三角困境：现有方法在可靠性、真实性和可扩展性上无法兼得。

Method: 核心思路是解耦感知与描述：使用确定性代码从真实开源多变量时间序列中计算一组统计量，再由LLM将这些预先计算的事实进行语言化描述。感知能力（LLM不擅长）由计算处理，而表达能力由LLM负责。基于此构建CGTime，一个4B参数的计算接地时间序列-语言模型。

Result: 在多变量理解任务上，CGTime超越了更大的通用模型：在保留基准上取得最优多变量事实分数（0.283，对比GPT-4o-mini的0.173和GPT-5.4-nano的0.203），且该差距通过了Holm校正的配对显著性检验。此外，在生成的描述中，CGTime能更准确地陈述可验证的数值事实，并覆盖更广泛的统计属性。

Conclusion: 通过将感知任务外包给确定性计算、让LLM专注于语言表达，可以同时实现可靠性、真实性和可扩展性三个目标，为解决时间序列-语言对齐中的自监督陷阱提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Training multimodal models to align time series with language runs into a self-supervision trap. The usual recipe asks an LLM to read a series and write a description, so label quality is capped by the perceptual skill the model is supposed to learn. The data can never teach more than the labeler already knows. A second gap makes this worse: most datasets use a single variable, but the patterns that matter (cross-channel correlation, lead-lag structure, co-occurring anomalies) appear only with several variables, right where the labeling LLM's limits are most exposed. These two problems create a trilemma: existing methods are reliable, realistic, or scalable, but none achieves all three. We resolve this by decoupling perception from description. Deterministic code computes a set of statistics from real, open-source multivariate series; the LLM verbalizes those precomputed facts. Perception, which LLMs do poorly, is handled by computation, while the LLM handles expression. This produces CGTime, our 4B-parameter computation-grounded time-series-language model. CGTime outperforms far larger general-purpose models on multivariate understanding tasks: it attains the best multivariate fact score on our held-out benchmark (0.283 vs. 0.173 for GPT-4o-mini and 0.203 for GPT-5.4-nano), a gap that survives Holm-corrected paired significance tests against every baseline. It also states verifiable numerical facts in generated captions more accurately and covers a broader range of statistical properties.

</details>


### [120] [Disentangling 3D Modeling from Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05242)
*Haoze Sun,Jiequan Cui,Qingshan Xu,Richang Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR)框架，将3D感知与空间推理明确解耦：先用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据，再用LoRA微调的LLM仅基于这些显式几何证据进行推理。无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具使用策略，就在主流空间推理基准上取得有竞争力的性能，同时具备更好的可解释性、模块化和计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统端到端方法通过大规模训练联合获取隐式的3D感知与推理能力，但作者观察到现代感知模型擅长估计连续3D几何，而LLM擅长组合性和符号性推理，二者互补。因此希望通过显式解耦感知与推理，探索一个可扩展且高效的空间智能替代范式。

Method: DiSR框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 使用现成的专家感知模型将物理世界重建为结构化3D证据；(2) 使用LoRA对LLM进行微调，使其仅基于这些显式几何证据进行空间推理，而非直接处理原始视觉输入。

Result: 在流行的空间推理基准上取得了与端到端方法相当的有竞争力性能，并且无需大规模3D VQA训练或复杂工具使用策略。此外，DiSR在可解释性、模块化和计算效率方面均有提升。

Conclusion: 显式分离感知与推理是空间智能的一种可扩展且有效的替代范式，证明了无需完全端到端建模也能实现强大的空间推理能力。

Abstract: In this work, we explore an alternative paradigm for spatial reasoning by explicitly disentangling 3D perception from reasoning, rather than jointly acquiring implicit 3D perception and reasoning through large-scale training. Our key observation is that modern perception models excel at estimating continuous 3D geometry, whereas large language models (LLMs) are particularly effective at compositional and symbolic reasoning. Motivated by these complementary strengths, we propose the Disentangled Spatial Reasoner (DiSR), a simple yet effective framework that reconstructs the physical world into structured 3D evidence using off-the-shelf expert perception models and fine-tunes an LLM with LoRA to perform reasoning solely over this explicit geometric evidence. Without large-scale 3D VQA training or complex tool-use policies, DiSR achieves competitive performance on popular spatial reasoning benchmarks. Beyond its strong performance, DiSR offers improved interpretability, modularity, and computational efficiency, demonstrating that explicit separation of perception and reasoning is a scalable and effective alternative paradigm to end-to-end modeling for spatial intelligence.

</details>


### [121] [Marginal Matching Does Not License Factorized Sampling: Auditing Conditional Style Leakage in Factorized Generative Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05243)
*Duong Bach,Hai Nguyen Hong,Cuong Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出因子化生成模型中常见的一种错误做法：仅将潜在风格变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验，就声称风格表示与类别信息独立。作者证明这是错误的，边缘匹配对类别条件分布无任何约束，风格潜在变量在整体呈高斯分布的同时仍可高度预测标签。通过精确分解，作者指出消除边缘-条件不匹配只是因子化采样四个必要条件之一，且不充分。实验表明，多个模型在近零全局MMD下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签；四种缓解策略能将探针准确率降至21%-46%；事后条件先验和风格库分别改善了条件生成质量。结论是：仅基于风格潜在变量边缘分布的散度无法证明其与类别标签独立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因子化生成模型广泛使用将潜在风格变量z_s的边缘分布匹配到固定高斯先验的做法，并将其解释为风格表示与类别信息独立的证据。作者质疑这一解释的正确性，旨在揭示边缘分布匹配不足以保证风格变量与类别标签的条件独立性，从而纠正这一常见误解。

Method: 作者首先推导出一个精确分解，证明边缘-条件分布不匹配只是因子化采样所需的四个条件之一。随后在案例研究模型和四个代表性潜在基线模型上进行实验，使用全局MMD评估边缘匹配程度，同时用线性探针和聚类准确率测量类别信息泄漏。此外，作者测试了六种独立扰动（模型容量、课程、先验几何、监督等）的稳健性，并评估了四种缓解策略以及两种事后补救方法（条件先验、经验风格库）。

Result: 案例研究模型和四个基线在近零全局MMD下，线性探针仍能以74%-100%的准确率恢复类别标签（10%的随机水平）。模型达到99.15%的聚类准确率，但外部评估的类别条件生成仅成功16%。六种扰动下泄漏仍然存在。四种缓解策略将探针准确率降至21%-46%，但类内依赖基本不变。事后条件先验使MNIST外部类别生成达到0.97，但CIFAR-10仅0.41；经验风格库在CIFAR-10上达到0.88。

Conclusion: 没有任何仅基于风格潜在变量边缘分布计算的散度能够证明其与类别标签独立。边缘统计的报告不足以验证因子化生成模型中所声称的属性。研究者需要更严格的约束（如条件分布层面的对齐）来保证真正的因子化采样。

Abstract: Factorized generative models commonly regularize a latent style variable z_s by matching its marginal distribution to a fixed Gaussian prior and interpret this as evidence that the style representation is independent of class information. We show that this interpretation is incorrect. Matching only the marginal distribution places no constraint on the class-conditional distributions, allowing the latent style to remain highly predictive of the label despite appearing perfectly Gaussian in aggregate. We derive an exact decomposition showing that this mismatch is one of four conditions required for factorized sampling, and demonstrate that eliminating it is necessary but not sufficient to obtain the intended factorization.
  Empirically, our case-study model and four representative latent baselines achieve near-zero global MMD while still allowing a linear probe to recover class labels with 74%--100% accuracy (10% chance level). Our model reaches 99.15% clustering accuracy, whereas externally evaluated class-conditional generation succeeds only 16% of the time. This leakage remains under six independent perturbations involving model capacity, curriculum, prior geometry, and supervision across two datasets. Four mitigation strategies reduce probe accuracy to 21%--46%, although they leave within-class dependence largely unchanged. A post-hoc conditional prior improves externally evaluated class generation to 0.97 on MNIST without retraining but reaches only 0.41 on CIFAR-10, while an empirical style bank achieves 0.88 on CIFAR-10. These results demonstrate that no divergence computed solely on the marginal distribution of the style latent can certify independence from class labels, and that reporting marginal statistics alone does not verify the property commonly claimed in factorized generative models.

</details>


### [122] [PRISM: Priority-aware Rubric Internalization via Structured Multimodal Data Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05249)
*Xiaomin He,Dongling Xiao,Jiahao Xie,Ruiqi Lu,Qianle Wang,Zhongbin Guo,Wanxuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架和PRISM-Eval评估基准，将多模态指令跟随重定义为"规则执行"任务（即rubric comprehension）。仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升至30.1%，且增益可迁移至四个开源MLLM（涵盖密集和MoE架构），且不损害通用基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的多模态指令往往捆绑多条优先级不等的要求，但现有多模态训练数据大多将指令跟随简化为回答一个自包含问题。这一差距限制了模型执行多规则、带优先级指令的能力。

Method: 提出rubric comprehension设定，将模型从"被评分者"转变为"执行者"：给定图像和类型化、带优先级的规则集，模型须逐条验证规则后给出整体判断。提出PRISM四阶段数据合成框架：（1）persona-task对生成，（2）前缀引导的规则集生成，（3）质量过滤的rubric，（4）结构化验证轨迹。提出PRISM-Eval，含Loose和Strict指标，使用确定性匹配固定标签，无需推理时评委模型。

Result: 仅用10K合成样本，PRISM将Qwen3-VL-4B的Strict准确率从9.5%提升到30.1%，且保持通用基准的平均表现；增益可迁移至四个额外开源MLLM（覆盖密集和MoE架构）。

Conclusion: 结构化rubric监督（structured rubric supervision）是通往多规则、优先级感知的多模态指令跟随的可扩展路径。

Abstract: Real-world multimodal instructions often bundle multiple requirements with unequal importance, yet most multimodal training data still reduce instruction following to answering one self-contained question. We study this gap through \textbf{rubric comprehension}, which casts the model not as a generator measured against rubrics but as an \textbf{executor} that follows them: given an image and a typed, prioritized rubric, the model must verify each rule before producing an overall judgment. To support this setting, we propose \textbf{PRISM}, a four-stage data synthesis framework that produces persona--task pairs, prefix-guided rule sets, quality-filtered rubrics, and structured verification traces. We further introduce \textbf{PRISM-Eval}, whose Loose and Strict metrics use deterministic matching against fixed labels and therefore require no inference-time judge model. With only 10K synthesized samples, PRISM lifts Qwen3-VL-4B from 9.5\% to 30.1\% Strict accuracy on PRISM-Eval while preserving average performance on general benchmarks, and the gains transfer to four additional open-source MLLMs across dense and MoE architectures, suggesting that structured rubric supervision is a scalable path toward multi-rule, priority-aware multimodal instruction following.

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Full-Model Rollback: AuroSFT for Adapter-State Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05250)
*Yue Han,Ziniu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AuroSFT是一种参数高效的过拟合感知多任务SFT框架，通过将调度器状态从全模型检查点重构为紧凑且可合并的适配器状态，在冻结预训练骨干网络、训练注入适配器的基础上，实现了任务级回滚与阶段的低成本切换，在五个骨干网络上均超越msft基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务监督微调(SFT)将异构数据混合视为单一优化问题，但不同任务在不同时刻达到最佳泛化。msft通过任务级roll-out、排斥和回滚来缓解此问题，但其原始实现将调度器状态物化为全模型检查点，导致阶段转换在存储、恢复和部署上成本高昂。

Method: AuroSFT冻结预训练骨干网络，仅训练注入的适配器；在任务级峰值时回滚适配器检查点，并继续在剩余活跃混合任务上训练。在层级别，每个适配器对低秩权重因子应用AuroRA启发的自适应非线性层（而非对样本表示）；更新保持对输入线性、秩有界，且可精确合并到冻结投影中。

Result: 在保留骨干网络对比协议下，AuroSFT平均准确率达到61.36%，优于对应msft参考行的59.85%，且在全部五个骨干网络上均取得更高准确率。

Conclusion: AuroSFT通过将可合并的适配器状态作为过拟合感知多任务SFT的载体状态，在保持性能优势的同时大幅降低了阶段转换的存储、恢复和部署成本，为多任务微调提供了参数高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-task supervised fine-tuning (SFT) often casts a heterogeneous data mixture as a single optimization problem, even though different tasks may reach their best generalization at different times. msft exposes this mismatch through task-wise roll-out, exclusion, and rollback, but its original formulation materializes the scheduler state as full-model checkpoints, making stage transitions costly to store, restore, and deploy. This paper introduces AuroSFT, a parameter-efficient framework that recasts the carried state of overfitting-aware multi-task SFT as a compact, mergeable adapter state. AuroSFT freezes the pretrained backbone, trains only injected adapters, rolls back adapter checkpoints at task-wise peaks, and continues on the remaining active mixture. At the layer level, each adapter applies an AuroRA-inspired adaptive nonlinear layer to a low-rank weight factor rather than to the sample representation. The resulting update remains linear in the input, rank-bounded, and exactly mergeable into the frozen projection. Under the retained-backbone comparison protocol, AuroSFT achieves 61.36% average accuracy, compared with 59.85% for the corresponding msft reference row, and obtains higher accuracy on all five backbones. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroSFT-80D1.

</details>


### [124] [Beyond Rotations: AuroOFT for Expressive Quantized Orthogonal Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05253)
*Yue Han,Dianlin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AuroOFT，在量化正交微调（QOFT）基础上附加零初始化的门控低秩非线性残差分支，使低比特语言模型在参数高效微调中具备非线性修正能力。在匹配协议下，AuroOFT相比QOFT在Macro-6上提升1.30-2.70%，相比QLoRA提升6.52-10.62%，同时节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QOFT通过学习结构化的激活旋转来实现低比特语言模型的参数高效微调，但其任务特定更新被限制在线性正交变换内，无法对输入进行依赖上下文的非线性修正，这限制了模型在下游任务上的适应能力。

Method: AuroOFT保留QOFT作为量化兼容的稳定正交分支，同时为每个适配的线性层附加一个零启动门控低秩非线性残差分支。该分支将激活映射到RMS归一化的紧凑潜空间，使用自适应非线性基并带有有界或token依赖的门控机制。零初始化的上投影确保AuroOFT在初始化时与QOFT在功能上完全一致，且正交性保持为分支级别的稳定属性而非组合非线性层的属性。

Result: 在匹配的数据、优化、解码和解析协议下，AuroOFT在1.5B/3B Qwen2.5设置上相比匹配的QOFT提升Macro-6达1.30-2.70%，超过QLoRA达6.52-10.62%，并在代表性规模下相对QLoRA节省32.3-44.7%的可训练参数。小规模的考试型选择题数学集仅作为协议敏感性诊断。

Conclusion: AuroOFT通过将QOFT的线性正交约束与零启动非线性残差结合，既保持了量化兼容的稳定性，又解锁了输入依赖的非线性修正能力，在性能与参数效率上均优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: Quantized orthogonal fine-tuning (qoft) enables parameter-efficient adaptation of low-bit language models by learning structured activation rotations before frozen quantized weights. However, its task-specific updates remain constrained to linear orthogonal transformations, limiting input-dependent nonlinear corrections. We introduce AuroOFT, which keeps qoft as a stable quantization-compatible branch while attaching a zero-start gated low-rank nonlinear residual to each adapted linear layer. AuroOFT maps activations into an RMS-normalized compact latent space and uses adaptive nonlinear bases with bounded or token-dependent gating. The zero-initialized up projection makes AuroOFT functionally identical to qoft at initialization, while orthogonality remains a branch-level stability property rather than a property of the combined nonlinear layer. Under matched data, optimization, decoding, and parser protocols, AuroOFT improves Macro-6 over matched qoft by 1.30-2.70% on the 1.5B/3B Qwen2.5 settings, exceeds QLoRA by 6.52-10.62%, and saves 32.3-44.7% trainable parameters relative to QLoRA in representative scales. The small exam-style multiple-choice math set is treated only as a protocol-sensitivity diagnostic. Our code is available at the anonymous repository: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/AuroOFT-F3FD.

</details>


### [125] [An Emerging Retail Portfolio Management Application: Personalized, Tax-Aware Reinforcement Learning with Natural Language Goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05255)
*Ramin Pishehvar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个面向散户投资者的端到端集成测试应用的完整构建：FastAPI后端+Web仪表盘，通过自然语言描述投资目标，路由至六大投资指令之一，并由三阶段强化学习系统（自监督跨资产编码器、带意图路由器的MoE分配策略、个性化LoRA适配器）生成实时的券商集成组合推荐。系统功能完备并通过Alpaca模拟盘API完成端到端集成测试，但尚未对真实用户开放，属于预部署应用，附带14天walk-forward回测和若干工程经验教训。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散户投资者缺乏机构客户才能享有的个性化、税务感知的投资组合管理服务：现有robo-advisor采用静态规则化配置，机构级系统又需要高资金门槛和复杂技术栈。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建一个FastAPI后端和Web仪表盘，用户以自然语言描述投资目标后被路由至六个投资指令之一；核心是三相强化学习系统：(1)自监督跨资产编码器，(2)带学习型意图路由器的Mixture-of-Experts(MoE)分配策略，(3)从个人券商交易行为中个性化推荐且无需重训共享模型的轻量LoRA适配器。系统包含多用户认证、预览-确认流程、每日邮件摘要和可审计的操作完整性链，并通过实时券商API（Alpaca模拟盘）进行端到端集成测试。

Result: 系统功能完整并通过端到端集成测试；提供了14天walk-forward回测（含bootstrap置信区间）作为预部署验证而非生产性能证明。同时总结了若干工程经验：静默失活的集成路径、第三方API调用挂起问题，以及端到端实证验证优于信任检查点元数据等教训。

Conclusion: 该系统是一个诚实标注的、部署前的应用原型，已具备明确的全量部署路径。作者强调回测结果仅作为初步验证而非生产性能指标，并认为其总结的工程经验可推广至其他基于外部实时数据源的强化学习系统。

Abstract: Retail investors lack access to the kind of personalized, tax-aware portfolio management that institutional clients take for granted -- existing robo-advisors use static, rule-based allocation, and institutional-grade systems require account minimums and technology stacks unavailable to individual investors. We present a fully built, integration-tested application that closes this gap: a FastAPI backend and web dashboard that let a user describe an investment goal in plain language (e.g. "I want steady growth but need to sell some shares next month for a down payment"), routes that goal to one of six investment mandates, and produces a live, broker-integrated portfolio recommendation from athree-phase reinforcement learning system -- a self-supervised cross-asset encoder, a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) allocation policy with a learned intent router, and a lightweight LoRA adapter that personalizes recommendations from an individual's revealed brokerage behavior without retraining the shared model. The system is functionally complete and integration-tested end-to-end against a live brokerage API (Alpaca, paper-trading mode), including multi-user authentication, a trust first preview-before-apply confirmation flow, daily email digests, and an auditable action-integrity chain, but has not yet been opened to real end-users; we report this honestly as an emerging, pre-deployment application with a concrete path to full deployment, alongside 14-day walk-forward backtests (bootstrapped confidence intervals included) as preliminary, pre-deployment validation rather than production performance. We also report several practical engineering lessons -- silently-inactive integration paths, hanging third-party API calls, and the value of end-to-end empirical verification over trusting checkpoint metadata -- that we believe generalize to other applied RL systems built on external, live data sources.

</details>


### [126] [FlowAdam: Implicit Regularization via Geometry-Aware Soft Momentum Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.06652)
*Devender Singh,Tarun Sheel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FlowAdam，一种将Adam与连续梯度流ODE积分结合的混合优化器，通过Soft Momentum Injection机制在Adam动量与ODE速度之间平滑过渡，在耦合参数优化任务（低秩矩阵/张量恢复、协同过滤）上显著降低泛化误差，同时保持良好条件数任务（CIFAR-10）上与原版Adam持平的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adam等自适应矩方法使用基于指数移动平均的逐坐标对角预条件器，该对角缩放依赖坐标系选择，在处理矩阵分解、张量分解和图神经网络等存在密集或旋转参数耦合的任务时，因独立处理每个参数而性能受限。

Method: FlowAdam通过常微分方程（ODE）引入连续梯度流积分来增强Adam。当EMA统计量检测到地形困难时，FlowAdam切换到裁剪后的ODE积分。核心贡献是Soft Momentum Injection，即在模式转换过程中将ODE速度与Adam动量进行混合，避免朴素混合方法导致的训练崩溃。

Result: 在耦合优化基准上，ODE积分提供隐式正则化，低秩矩阵/张量恢复任务的留出误差降低10-22%，Jester（真实协同过滤）数据集上降低6%，超越调优后的Lion和AdaBelief；在良条件任务（CIFAR-10）上与Adam持平。MovieLens-100K实验确认收益来源于耦合参数交互而非偏置估计。消融实验显示soft injection至关重要，硬替换将准确率从100%降至82.5%。

Conclusion: FlowAdam通过软动量注入实现了Adam与ODE梯度流的高效混合，既保留了Adam在良条件任务上的优势，又通过ODE积分捕获参数间的耦合关系，为处理旋转或密集参数耦合的优化问题提供了一种实用且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Adaptive moment methods such as Adam use a diagonal, coordinate-wise preconditioner based on exponential moving averages of squared gradients. This diagonal scaling is coordinate-system dependent and can struggle with dense or rotated parameter couplings, including those in matrix factorization, tensor decomposition, and graph neural networks, because it treats each parameter independently. We introduce FlowAdam, a hybrid optimizer that augments Adam with continuous gradient-flow integration via an ordinary differential equation (ODE). When EMA-based statistics detect landscape difficulty, FlowAdam switches to clipped ODE integration. Our central contribution is Soft Momentum Injection, which blends ODE velocity with Adam's momentum during mode transitions. This prevents the training collapse observed with naive hybrid approaches. Across coupled optimization benchmarks, the ODE integration provides implicit regularization, reducing held-out error by 10-22% on low-rank matrix/tensor recovery and 6% on Jester (real-world collaborative filtering), also surpassing tuned Lion and AdaBelief, while matching Adam on well-conditioned workloads (CIFAR-10). MovieLens-100K confirms benefits arise specifically from coupled parameter interactions rather than bias estimation. Ablation studies show that soft injection is essential, as hard replacement reduces accuracy from 100% to 82.5%.

</details>


### [127] [Evaluating Machine Learning Models for Post-Wildfire Debris-Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05265)
*Quinn Ledingham,Zhengsen Xu,Yimin Zhu,Zack Dewis,Mabel Heffring,Saeid Taleghanidoozdoozan,Motasem Alkayid,Megan Greenwood,Lincoln Linlin Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了15种机器学习模型（含TabPFN）用于预测美国西部野火后泥石流事件，发现TabPFN在无增强数据下取得最高威胁评分（0.637），SHAP分析显示短时降雨强度和暴雨累积量是最重要特征，并通过TabPFN生成合成数据增强有效提升了除CNN外所有模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 野火后泥石流预测对减轻灾害至关重要，但面临三大挑战：泥石流与非泥石流事件在特征空间中重叠、需要模型可解释性、训练数据有限。现有研究缺乏对这些挑战的系统性评估。

Method: 利用美国西部流域尺度的野火后泥石流观测数据，对比15种机器学习模型（含TabPFN）；采用重复分层交叉验证评估预测性能；使用SHAP进行特征重要性分析；使用TabPFN生成的合成样本进行数据增强评估。

Result: TabPFN在无增强数据下取得最高威胁评分0.637，紧随其后的是最佳树模型；SHAP分析表明短时降雨强度和暴雨累积量排名最高，燃烧严重度和地形特征贡献较小；合成数据增强除CNN外对所有模型均有提升，深度学习模型平均威胁评分最大提升+0.041。

Conclusion: 通过严格的模型基准测试、可解释的特征分析和合成数据增强，本文为改进野火后泥石流预测提供了综合框架，证明了TabPFN在该任务中的优越性以及合成数据增强对缓解数据稀缺问题的有效性。

Abstract: Prediction of post-wildfire debris flows is critical for mitigating hazards to communities, infrastructure, and resources during intense rainfall in recently burned areas. However, identifying reliable machine learning models is complicated by overlapping debris-flow and non-debris-flow events in feature space, the need for model interpretability, and limited training data. This paper addresses these challenges through a systematic evaluation of machine learning models in terms of predictive performance, feature importance, and synthetic data augmentation. Using basin-scale observations of post-wildfire debris-flow events across the western United States, we compare 15 models, including the Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Network (TabPFN). Repeated stratified cross-validation shows that TabPFN achieves the highest unaugmented performance with a threat score of 0.637, closely followed by the best tree-based models. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) are used to identify the features driving predictions, revealing that short-duration rainfall intensity and storm accumulation consistently rank highest, while burn severity and terrain features contribute less. We further evaluate synthetic data augmentation using TabPFN-generated samples to address the scarcity of debris-flow observations. Synthetic augmentation improves the performance of all models except CNN, with the largest mean threat score increase of +0.041 among the deep learning models. By combining rigorous model benchmarking, interpretable feature analysis, and synthetic data augmentation, this work provides a comprehensive framework for improving post-wildfire debris-flow prediction.

</details>


### [128] [The Loss Does Not See the Basis, but Adam Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05136)
*Devender Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了优化器在低秩归纳偏置上的本质差异：梯度下降等优化器因满足规范等变性（gauge-equivariance）而隐含偏置低秩解，而Adam、RMSProp等坐标式方法不满足该条件，导致失去低秩恢复能力。文章通过结构定理、传输定理以及多项实验系统验证了这一理论框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解不同优化器对低秩矩阵恢复的归纳偏置差异。梯度下降在分解模型上隐含偏置低秩解，但Adam从相同初始化出发却不具备此性质，作者希望追溯这一差异的根本原因。

Method: 从损失函数的规范对称性（gauge symmetry）出发，论证优化器需具备规范等变性才能继承梯度流的低秩机制。提出结构定理刻画无记忆等变规则恰为Gram决定的左预条件子，传输定理将梯度流的路径性质推广到公共标量流。构建从坐标式到共享标量预条件的单参数族，并设计"谱调度"解释Muon的矛盾结果，最后在矩阵感知、Transformer和超光谱数据集上进行验证。

Result: 在欠定矩阵感知中，从坐标式到共享标量预条件的单参数族单调恢复低秩偏置，证明各向异性（anisotropy）是根源。Muon在等速率更新时能精确恢复低秩目标，但随谱尾增长失去优势。Transformer中Adam在第一步就分离两个规范等价初始化，最终per-head不变量相对Frobenius距离相差56%。超光谱数据集上梯度下降在最低采样密度下将留出误差降低43-44%。

Conclusion: 基（basis）的选择不是调参细节，而是决定优化器选择哪个插值器（interpolant）的关键决策。规范等变性是优化器继承梯度流低秩机制的必要而非充分条件，各向异性是造成坐标式方法失去低秩偏置的根本原因。

Abstract: Gradient descent on a factored model $W = UV^\top$ is implicitly biased toward low-rank solutions, while Adam, starting from the same small initialization, is not. We trace the difference to the gauge symmetry of the loss, its invariance under $(U, V) \mapsto (UQ, VQ)$. Gradient flow's low-rank mechanism is available to an optimizer only if that optimizer is gauge-equivariant, a condition necessary for the transfer but not sufficient for low-rank recovery. Gradient descent, momentum, "shared-scalar" Adam, Muon, and Shampoo satisfy it. Adam, RMSProp, and the other coordinate-wise methods do not. A structure theorem characterizes the memoryless equivariant rules as exactly the Gram-determined left preconditioners, and a transfer theorem carries gradient flow's pathwise properties to common-scalar flows. We then sort nine update rules on underdetermined matrix sensing by recovery error against the planted ground truth. A one-parameter family from coordinate-wise to shared-scalar preconditioning restores the bias monotonically, isolating anisotropy as the cause. A "spectral schedule" reconciles two opposing reports about Muon: equal-rate updates recover exactly low-rank targets but lose their edge as the spectral tail grows. In transformers, Adam separates two gauge-equivalent initializations at the first step, where the equivariant optimizers stay at float precision, and ends with the per-head invariants $W_Q^\top W_K$ 56% apart in relative Frobenius distance, a gap no per-head rotation can close. On two hyperspectral datasets at matched training loss, gradient descent cuts held-out error by 43-44% at the lowest sampling density, and at lower effective rank. Basis choice is therefore not a tuning detail but a decision about which interpolant the optimizer selects.

</details>


### [129] [Rectifying Geometric Misalignment: Online Source-Free Adaptation for Class-Imbalanced EEG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05315)
*Shiwen Chu,Shanglin Li,Motoaki Kawanabe,Reinmar Kobler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出OSPDIM，一种基于SPD流形的无源在线无监督域自适应框架，通过信息最大化优化流形约束的偏置参数来校正类标签偏移导致的几何错位，提升EEG-BCI跨被试/会话的在线泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于EEG的BCI系统常需无监督域自适应以泛化到新被试和新会话。虽然黎曼对齐方法（如RCT）能处理协变量偏移，但它们隐含假设类别先验平衡。在现实在线BCI场景中，标签分布动态变化（标签偏移），导致标准对齐技术在几何上错位目标数据分布。

Method: OSPDIM在切空间映射中引入流形约束的偏置参数，通过信息最大化优化该参数以纠正数据流不平衡引起的几何偏斜。与依赖全局批次统计的离线方法不同，OSPDIM能够在线实时估计并纠正几何偏置。

Result: 在2D SPD矩阵上的仿真直观展示了OSPDIM成功纠正了标准中心化方法无法解决的错位问题。在多个运动想象数据集上的大量实验表明，OSPDIM显著优于标准黎曼基线，尤其是在类别不平衡严重的挑战性在线自适应场景中。

Conclusion: OSPDIM为实际可插拔的BCI系统提供了鲁棒解决方案，有效应对在线场景中的类别不平衡和标签偏移问题。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) based Brain-Computer Interfaces (BCIs) often require unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) to generalize across subjects and sessions. While Riemannian alignment methods like the Riemannian Centering Transformation (RCT) are effective for handling covariate shifts, they implicitly assume balanced class priors. However, in realistic online BCI scenarios, the label distributions vary dynamically (label shift), causing standard alignment techniques to geometrically misalign the target data distributions. In this work, we propose OSPDIM (Online SPD manifold information maximization), a source-free online UDA framework designed to address label shifts on the Riemannian manifold. OSPDIM introduces a manifold-constrained bias parameter into the tangent space mapping, which is optimized via information maximization to correct the geometric skew caused by imbalanced data streams. Unlike offline methods relying on global batch statistics, OSPDIM estimates and corrects geometric bias on-the-fly. Simulations on 2D SPD matrices visually demonstrate that OSPDIM successfully rectifies the misalignment where standard centering fails. Extensive experiments on multiple motor imagery datasets show that OSPDIM significantly outperforms standard Riemannian baselines, particularly in challenging online adaptation scenarios with severe class imbalance, offering a robust solution for practical, plug-and-play BCI systems.

</details>


### [130] [QEvict: Recoverable Quantized KV Eviction for Attention-Drift-Robust Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05326)
*Ayushman Garg,Akshita Gupta,Shaswata Bhattacharya,Abhishek Gupta,Sandeep Kumar,Manoj Kumar*
